### K-F4 · `cmd/tether/cluster_backup.go:276`

- **lane**：deploy and release surface (batch K): scripts/install.sh, internal/proto.SameRelease and its call sites, cmd/tether/node.go, cmd/tether/cluster_upgrade_drive.go, internal/clusterupgrade/plan.go, internal/broker/reexec.go, docs/broker-ops.md, docs/usage.md, test/p10/config_preservation_test.go
- **裁决**：CONFIRMED（原报 MAJOR → 订正 MINOR）
- **主张**：A product string printed to an operator mid-disaster-recovery still asserts the behaviour this change removed, and cmd/tether/restore_test.go:422 pins that false sentence in place.
- **失败场景**：A fresh DR box: `tether cluster recovery restore` has just applied the broker.cluster seam to broker.yaml, /etc/tether/nats.d/nats.conf does not exist (the os.Stat at line 267 fails). The command prints step 1a telling the operator to hand-write the stock conf and then, verbatim: "Do NOT re-run install.sh now: it OVERWRITES broker.yaml AND nats.conf and removes the broker.cluster seam this restore just applied." As of this change both halves are false. nats.conf is MISSING on that box, so config_dest takes the `[ ! -e ]` branch and install.sh would write exactly the stock conf the text asks the operator to reproduce by hand; broker.yaml EXISTS, so install.sh keeps it and its seam and writes broker.yaml.new. The guidance now steers a stressed operator away from the simplest correct action, on a premise the same commit falsified. The source comment at :265 ("install.sh unconditionally overwrites broker.yaml + nats.conf, destroying the seam this restore just applied") is false for the same reason.
- **verifier 理由**：The factual claim holds. cluster_backup.go:276-277 prints 'Do NOT re-run install.sh now: it OVERWRITES broker.yaml AND nats.conf and removes the broker.cluster seam this restore just applied', the :265 source comment says the same, and cmd/tether/restore_test.go:422 asserts the literal string — so a guard now pins a claim this change falsified. I confirmed the premise: scripts/install.sh:152-161 config_dest takes the real path only when the file is absent or --force-config, and :798 (broker.yaml) / :1041 (nats.d/nats.conf) both route through it; on the described DR box broker.yaml exists (kept, .new written beside) and nats.conf is missing (written). cluster_backup.go is not in the diff, so the site was indeed never revisited. Severity downgraded from MAJOR: the guidance is now over-conservative, not wrong-direction — the operator who follows it does more manual work and reaches a correc
- **对修法的批评**：The proposed replacement text is unsafe. A bare `install.sh --role broker` re-run is not 'the simplest correct action' on a DR box: install_broker (scripts/install.sh:775-791) runs place_binary and replaces /usr/local/bin/tether with the LATEST release, plus install_nats_server and install_caddy — an uncoordinated binary swap in the middle of a restore, on a fleet whose only sanctioned upgrade path is the staged, leader-last `cluster upgrade`. Rewrite the note to correct the REASON (a bare re-run keeps broker.yaml and writes broker.yaml.new; only --force-config clobbers the seam) while keeping a caution, or scope the suggestion to `install.sh --role broker --skip-download`. Updating restore_test.go's assertion in lockstep is right, and the new sentence should be pinned the same way so the guard keeps tracking behaviour.
- **建议修法**：Rewrite the step-1a note to the post-DRD-F1 truth: a bare re-run creates the missing nats.conf and KEEPS broker.yaml (writing broker.yaml.new beside it), and only `--force-config` would clobber the seam. Update the :265 comment with it, and change the restore_test.go assertion to the new sentence (with `--force-config` named), so the guard tracks the behaviour instead of the old claim.

### K-F5 · `docs/broker-ops.md:79`

- **lane**：deploy and release surface (batch K): scripts/install.sh, internal/proto.SameRelease and its call sites, cmd/tether/node.go, cmd/tether/cluster_upgrade_drive.go, internal/clusterupgrade/plan.go, internal/broker/reexec.go, docs/broker-ops.md, docs/usage.md, test/p10/config_preservation_test.go
- **裁决**：CONFIRMED（原报 MAJOR → 订正 MINOR）
- **主张**：Two live statements in broker-ops.md still assert the pre-fix overwrite behaviour, in the same file whose §8.5 and §8.6 were updated — the contract-change sweep stopped at the two sections the author was editing.
- **失败场景**：docs/broker-ops.md is tier-2 authority (CLAUDE.md §1). Line 79, inside the #76/#77 retrofit box, says: '不要在存量机上重跑 install.sh(会覆写 broker.yaml/Caddyfile);一次性手动补齐:' and then hands the operator a five-command manual recipe. That reason is now false, and worse, the recipe is now unnecessary: a bare re-run keeps broker.yaml and the Caddyfile AND performs the #76 `systemctl enable` and writes the #77 journald drop-in. An operator following the doc does a fiddly manual retrofit that install.sh would do correctly. Line 810 ('在已有部署上（例如 racknerd）逐步做，别重跑 install.sh——它会覆盖你手工维护的 cluster: 段') tells the same falsehood for the h1 migration. Both remain in the file after §8.5 (line 708) and §8.6 (line 744) were rewritten for exactly this change.
- **verifier 理由**：Both sites verified unchanged in the diff and now false. docs/broker-ops.md:79 reads '不要在存量机上重跑 install.sh(会覆写 broker.yaml/Caddyfile)' and :810 reads '别重跑 install.sh——它会覆盖你手工维护的 cluster: 段'; both broker.yaml (:798) and the Caddyfile (:861) route through config_dest, so a bare re-run keeps them. The same file's §8.5 (:708) and §8.6 (:744) WERE rewritten for exactly this change, so the sweep did stop at the sections being edited — the reviewer's characterisation is accurate, and broker-ops.md is tier-2 authority. Severity downgraded from MAJOR for the same reason as K-F4: the advice remains directionally safe (the operator does a manual retrofit that works); only its stated reason is stale.
- **对修法的批评**：The proposed rewrite is the dangerous half of this finding. 'A bare re-run is now safe and is the preferred way to pick up the #76 enable + #77 drop-in' is false in a way that matters more than the sentence it replaces: on an existing production broker a re-run replaces /usr/local/bin/tether, nats-server and caddy with the latest release (place_binary / install_nats_server / install_caddy all run before the config writes), and enable_broker_units runs `systemctl daemon-reload` + `systemctl enable` on a live host. Correct the REASON at :79 and :810 and keep the manual recipe as the recommendation; if a re-run is to be endorsed at all it must be spelled `--skip-download --version <the version already installed>`. The follow-up re-grep of docs/ and cmd/ for remaining clobber claims is the right instinct and is the part worth keeping.
- **建议修法**：Rewrite :79 to say a bare re-run is now safe and is the preferred way to pick up the #76 enable + #77 drop-in, keeping the manual recipe only for hosts that cannot re-run the installer; rewrite :810 the same way with a pointer to --force-config for the deliberate-overwrite case. Then re-grep docs/ and cmd/ for every remaining install.sh-clobber claim (K-F4 is the third one).

### K-F6 · `cmd/tether/node_versions.go:61`

- **lane**：deploy and release surface (batch K): scripts/install.sh, internal/proto.SameRelease and its call sites, cmd/tether/node.go, cmd/tether/cluster_upgrade_drive.go, internal/clusterupgrade/plan.go, internal/broker/reexec.go, docs/broker-ops.md, docs/usage.md, test/p10/config_preservation_test.go
- **裁决**：CONFIRMED（原报 MINOR → 订正 MINOR）
- **主张**：The #19 dual-version verdict — the table an operator reads to decide whether a host is upgraded — compares releases with raw != / ==, the third and fourth sites the SameRelease census missed.
- **失败场景**：Line 61 `row.Skew = ... && d.BrokerVer != av` is live via `tether node ls --brokers` (renderNodeLsBrokers → correlateBrokerVersions). A host whose broker daemon was built from source with `make build VERSION=v0.5.1` (Makefile:5-6 keeps the v) alongside an agent installed from the release tarball (goreleaser strips it → "0.5.1") shows SKEW=true, whose documented meaning is 'whole-host upgrade is INCOMPLETE'. That is the same premise SameRelease exists for, aimed at the operator's dashboard. Line 63 `row.WholeHostAt = d.BrokerVer == target && ok && av == target` has the identical defect twice; it is currently only reachable from tests (the sole production caller passes target ""), so it is latent rather than live — but its own field comment says 'both == target (the single #19 "done" criterion)'.
- **verifier 理由**：Verified: node_versions.go:61 `row.Skew = d.BrokerVer != "" && ok && av != "" && d.BrokerVer != av` and :63 `row.WholeHostAt = d.BrokerVer == target && ok && av == target`. The reviewer's own reachability disclosure is honest and correct — renderNodeLsBrokers at :97 is the sole production caller and passes target "", so WholeHostAt is test-only (latent) while Skew is live via `node ls --brokers`. The mixed-prefix pair is reachable for the same goreleaser-vs-Makefile reason as K-F1. node_versions_test.go uses "v2" on both sides throughout, so neither line is covered. MINOR is the right severity: the harm is one misleading dashboard column, not an action taken by the system.
- **对修法的批评**：Correct, and the existing guard `d.BrokerVer != "" && ok && av != ""` already prevents SameRelease("","")==true from turning two unknowns into a false 'same'. Add one thing the finding omits: the field comment at node_versions.go:35 says 'both == target (the single #19 "done" criterion)' and must be reworded with the code, or it becomes the next K-F12. Since Skew is the only live half, that is where the mutation-verification should be aimed.
- **建议修法**：Use proto.SameRelease for the skew test (`!proto.SameRelease(d.BrokerVer, av)`) and for both halves of WholeHostAt, and add a correlateBrokerVersions case with BrokerVer "v0.5.1" / AgentVer "0.5.1" asserting Skew==false and WholeHostAt==true against target "v0.5.1".

### K-F7 · `scripts/install.sh:480`

- **lane**：deploy and release surface (batch K): scripts/install.sh, internal/proto.SameRelease and its call sites, cmd/tether/node.go, cmd/tether/cluster_upgrade_drive.go, internal/clusterupgrade/plan.go, internal/broker/reexec.go, docs/broker-ops.md, docs/usage.md, test/p10/config_preservation_test.go
- **裁决**：CONFIRMED（原报 MINOR → 订正 MINOR）
- **主张**：Both closing banners now make a claim the same change falsified: they announce files as written/created that this run deliberately did not write.
- **失败场景**：Second `install.sh --role agent --broker <NEW-URL> --session lab --nid lab-1` on a host that already has agent.yaml. Observed output, verbatim: '✔ agent config written to /tmp/lanehome/.tether/agent/lab/agent.yaml' followed five lines later by the start command showing the NEW broker URL — while the file on disk still names the old one. report_kept_configs does print the correction afterwards, but the ✔ line above it is a positive assertion of the opposite. The broker banner has the same defect at line 908: '✔ systemd units created and ENABLED for boot' is printed on a re-run where the three units were KEPT and only *.service.new were written; what got enabled is the old units. This is the class the repo's own memory names (feedback-contract-change-sweep: 含产品打印给运维的手抄文案).
- **verifier 理由**：Reproduced directly. A second `install.sh --role agent` under an isolated HOME printed '✔ agent config written to /tmp/.../agent.yaml' and only afterwards the '⚠ EXISTING CONFIG KEPT' block naming that same path — the ✔ line is inside an unconditional heredoc at :480 and never consults CONFIG_DEST. The broker half is the same shape: :908 'systemd units created and ENABLED for boot' sits in the ENABLED_UNITS branch, which knows nothing about KEPT_CONFIGS, so a re-run that kept all four unit/conf files still claims they were created. One evidence error, immaterial to the finding: the agent start command does NOT show the broker URL (it prints only --session/--nid), so 'the start command showing the NEW broker URL while the file on disk names the old one' is wrong; the ✔ line alone is the defect.
- **对修法的批评**：Recording AGENT_CONFIG_DEST works — the agent banner is already an unquoted heredoc, so interpolating the real destination is free. The broker half needs care the finding does not mention: those three banners are QUOTED heredocs by an explicit documented decision at :894-896 ('Quoting keeps a future edit that adds a $ or a backtick from silently expanding — or executing — under root'). Select among literal quoted heredocs on a kept/not-kept flag; do not unquote them to interpolate. Also note test/p10/install_sh_test.go:136 asserts the literal 'agent config written to' on the fresh-install path, so the first-install wording must survive the change.
- **建议修法**：Record the agent's actual destination (e.g. set AGENT_CONFIG_DEST=$CONFIG_DEST) and print '✔ agent config written to <dest>' with the real path, or '✔ agent config KEPT at … (new content in agent.yaml.new)'. For the broker, make the three banner branches say 'systemd units written' only when none were kept, else 'units already present and left as-is; new content in *.service.new'. Extend TestInstallShAgentRerun to assert the banner does not claim the real path was written when it was kept.

### K-F8 · `scripts/install.sh:943`

- **lane**：deploy and release surface (batch K): scripts/install.sh, internal/proto.SameRelease and its call sites, cmd/tether/node.go, cmd/tether/cluster_upgrade_drive.go, internal/clusterupgrade/plan.go, internal/broker/reexec.go, docs/broker-ops.md, docs/usage.md, test/p10/config_preservation_test.go
- **裁决**：CONFIRMED（原报 MINOR → 订正 MINOR）
- **主张**：report_kept_configs is the last statement of each installer, so the two documented die() paths between the config writes and the report swallow the whole kept-config report.
- **失败场景**：Broker re-run on a host where the units and nats.conf already exist. write_systemd_units (line 1038-1146) keeps all four files and writes four .new files. enable_broker_units then runs; `systemctl enable` fails (a common real case: no polkit, a masked unit, a running-but-degraded systemd) and line 630 calls die, which exits 1 immediately. report_kept_configs at line 943 never runs. The operator sees only 'systemctl enable failed — units are written but the broker would NOT come back after a reboot' — a message that is itself wrong (the units were NOT written, they were kept) and is never followed by the list of four .new files sitting on disk. The same hole exists for any failure inside write_journald_dropin (line 892, mkdir -p under set -e).
- **verifier 理由**：Structurally verified: `set -eu` at :34; write_systemd_units populates KEPT_CONFIGS at :1041-:1146; enable_broker_units runs next with die at :628 and :630; report_kept_configs is only reached at :943, after the banner. So any die (or any unguarded failure under set -e) between the config writes and the banner drops the entire kept-config report, and the die text 'units are written but the broker would NOT come back after a reboot' is itself wrong on a re-run where the units were kept. The agent path has the identical shape with its single call at :496. MINOR is right — the files are still correct on disk; only the report is lost.
- **对修法的批评**：The proposed `trap report_kept_configs EXIT` is silently broken for the broker role and would ship as a dead guard. install_nats_server (:978) and install_caddy (:1007) each already set their own `trap "rm -rf '$tmp'" EXIT` and then clear it with `trap - EXIT` (:986, :1015) — and both run BEFORE the broker's config writes (install_broker calls them at :791-792, broker.yaml is written at :798). An EXIT trap installed at KEPT_CONFIGS init is therefore overwritten and then removed before a single file is kept. Either chain the traps through a helper that preserves the tmpdir cleanup, or (simpler and testable) call report_kept_configs from die() and keep the banner call. Whichever way, the p10 test must force a failure AFTER the writes, or it will pass against the broken version too.
- **建议修法**：Install a trap: `trap report_kept_configs EXIT` (guarded so it fires once) set right after KEPT_CONFIGS is initialised, and drop the two explicit calls — then every exit path that recorded a kept file reports it, including die and set -e. Cover it with a p10 test that forces a failure after the writes and asserts EXISTING CONFIG KEPT still appears.

### K-F9 · `scripts/install.sh:152`

- **lane**：deploy and release surface (batch K): scripts/install.sh, internal/proto.SameRelease and its call sites, cmd/tether/node.go, cmd/tether/cluster_upgrade_drive.go, internal/clusterupgrade/plan.go, internal/broker/reexec.go, docs/broker-ops.md, docs/usage.md, test/p10/config_preservation_test.go
- **裁决**：CONFIRMED（原报 MINOR → 订正 MINOR）
- **主张**：Nothing ever cleans up a .new file, so --force-config inverts the banner's own promise and --uninstall leaves three unit .new files behind forever.
- **失败场景**：(a) Run install.sh --role agent; run it again (agent.yaml.new written with content X); run it a third time with --force-config and a new --broker (agent.yaml now holds Y, agent.yaml.new still holds X). Verified on disk: agent.yaml has `broker_url: wss://NEW.example.com:443` while agent.yaml.new has `broker_url: wss://b.example.com:443`. An operator who later does the exact thing the banner told them to do — `diff agent.yaml agent.yaml.new` — is shown a diff pointing backwards, with the stale file labelled '.new'. (b) uninstall_broker removes `/etc/systemd/system/$u.service` at line 1172 but not `$u.service.new`, so three files survive an uninstall in /etc/systemd/system with no owner and no marker; a later reinstall writes fresh real units next to them and they persist indefinitely. (/etc/tether/*.new are collected by the `rm -rf /etc/tether` at :1196, so only the unit files leak.)
- **verifier 理由**：Half (a) reproduced on disk: after install → re-run → `--force-config` with a new --broker, agent.yaml holds `broker_url: wss://FORCED.example.com:443` while agent.yaml.new still holds `wss://NEW.example.com:443` — the file labelled '.new' is older than the real one, and the banner's own instruction to diff them points backwards. Half (b) verified by reading: install.sh:1171-1173 removes only `/etc/systemd/system/$u.service`, while :1063/:1089/:1131 write `$u.service.new` through config_dest, so three orphans survive an uninstall (the /etc/tether ones are swept by the `rm -rf /etc/tether` at :1196, as the reviewer correctly notes). I also confirmed test/simcluster/drills/32-install-lifecycle.sh has no .new assertion at all, so neither tier would notice.
- **对修法的批评**：Both edits are right in shape. Two refinements: (1) `rm -f "${_cd_path}.new"` in the force branch fires before the heredoc write, so a failed write leaves neither the new content nor the diff basis — do the removal after a successful write, or accept it as negligible and say so; (2) the finding only covers the force branch, but the `[ ! -e "$_cd_path" ]` branch has the same staleness (a .new outliving a deleted real file), so clear it in both branches. The drill-32 predicate change is the cheapest real coverage here since that tier already runs the broker install as root.
- **建议修法**：In config_dest's force branch, `rm -f "${_cd_path}.new"` when overwriting (the .new is by definition superseded). In uninstall_broker's loop, also `rm -f /etc/systemd/system/$u.service.new`. Assert both in test/p10/config_preservation_test.go (a --force-config run leaves no .new) and in drill 32's units-gone predicate.

### L-F2 · `internal/proc/proc.go:204`

- **lane**：Batch E / B5 — the cross-session process-exit fence (internal/proc/proc.go, internal/proc/plan.go, internal/broker/{exec,clusterwrite,cluster_forward}.go, internal/proc/exit_session_fence_test.go)
- **裁决**：PARTIAL（原报 MINOR → 订正 MINOR）
- **主张**：The comment 'a foreign pid is indistinguishable from an unknown one … the caller's terminal ack tells the sender nothing about whether the pid exists' is false — exec.go's two pre-reads are still sid-unscoped, so the ack is a working cross-session PID existence oracle; and this is the half of plan B5's adjudicated fix ('复用 exit 臂已有的预读做归属检查，只收紧到 SID') that was not applied.
- **失败场景**：An agent in session 'evil' publishes on its OWN legal subject `tether.v2.s.evil.ev.node.e1.proc.<pid>.started`. exec.go:145 reads `proc.Get(b.read().SQL(), pid)` with no sid, so it answers `already_recorded` when the pid belongs to ANY session and `recorded` when it does not — a complete cross-session probe for 'does this ULID exist anywhere on this broker'. The exit arm (exec.go:191) leaks the same way in one state: `already_exited` for a foreign pid that has exited vs `unknown_pid` for one that does not exist. The write fence itself holds (no row is mutated, no audit is emitted), so the impact is disclosure only — but the comment asserting the opposite is load-bearing in a repo that treats comments as the record of what a fence achieves, and the next person hardening this handler will read it and stop.
- **verifier 理由**：The empirical half reproduces exactly. My own scratch test against the repo's procAckHarness (publishing on subject sid='evil') got: exit/foreign-RUNNING → {OK:true unknown_pid}, exit/foreign-EXITED → {OK:true already_exited}, exit/ghost → {OK:true unknown_pid}, started/foreign → {OK:true already_recorded}, started/ghost → {OK:true recorded}; victim row stayed RUNNING/lab. exec.go:145 and :191 are indeed proc.Get's only callers and proc.Get's predicate is `WHERE pid = ?` alone (proc.go:219-224). So the sentence at proc.go:204 ('the caller's terminal ack tells the sender nothing about whether the pid exists') is overbroad and should be corrected. But two of the finding's framings do not survive. (1) 'This is the half of B5's adjudicated fix that was not applied' is a mischaracterization: plan line 149 states the DEFECT as the cross-session write escape ending in a SIGKILL, and names narro
- **对修法的批评**：(a) is the right call and is free. (b) is worse than the reviewer says, in the mode they did not check. They critique the started arm via recordProc's `INSERT OR IGNORE` — that is PlanInsert (plan.go:103), the CLUSTER path. In SINGLE mode recordProc calls proc.Insert (proc.go:118-124), which is a plain `INSERT INTO processes` inside a tx: a foreign pid falling through hits the pid PRIMARY KEY, returns a wrapped constraint error, and exec.go acks {OK:false, store_error} — which under the h1 C3 contract means the agent's courier retries that event FOREVER. That is a self-inflicted retry storm traded for an oracle that needs a ULID the attacker must already possess. In cluster mode it is additionally an attacker-driven stream of OpProcCreate raft proposals that all IGNORE. If anything is narrowed, narrow only the EXIT arm (`existing.SID == sid` before the already_exited short-circuit) — tha
- **建议修法**：Either (a) correct the comment to state what is actually true — the UPDATE and the existence probe are fenced, the ack CODE still discloses pid existence, and that was accepted — or (b) narrow both pre-reads, which is what B5 adjudicated. If you take (b), the exit arm is trivial (`existing.SID == sid` before the already_exited short-circuit; a foreign pid then falls through to markProcExited and gets the same unknown_pid it would for a ghost). The started arm needs care and is the reason to think twice: skipping the short-circuit for a foreign pid lets control reach recordProc, whose `INSERT OR IGNORE` on the pid PRIMARY KEY is a silent no-op, after which exec.go:181 would publish audit.proc

### L-F3 · `internal/broker/clusterwrite.go:1227`

- **lane**：Batch E / B5 — the cross-session process-exit fence (internal/proc/proc.go, internal/proc/plan.go, internal/broker/{exec,clusterwrite,cluster_forward}.go, internal/proc/exit_session_fence_test.go)
- **裁决**：PARTIAL（原报 MINOR → 订正 MINOR）
- **主张**：Two comments written by this commit state the forwarded fence as an unconditional guarantee and name the wrong function for it; both read as assurances the code does not provide.
- **失败场景**：clusterwrite.go:1227 says the sid 'rides the forward payload so the LEADER applies the same fence the single-node writer does; a fence that only held on the mode the attacker did not happen to hit would not be one.' That is true only while the payload is populated, and — unlike proc.MarkExited, which fails closed on an empty sid — PlanMarkExited silently degrades to the pre-fence predicate instead. cluster_forward.go:217-218 compounds it: 'Once every broker is on this release the field is always present and the cross-session fence (proc.MarkExited) holds on the forwarded path too.' proc.MarkExited is NEVER called on the forwarded path (its own doc at proc.go:170-176 says it is single-mode only and it would reject the very empty sid the hatch exists for); the forwarded fence is proc.PlanMarkExited, the one function that accepts an empty sid. A reader auditing the cluster path follows the named function, finds the fail-closed check, and concludes the forwarded path is fail-closed too. Combined with L-F1 (nothing asserts the payload carries sid), the comments are the only thing standing where a guard should be.
- **verifier 理由**：Two halves, and only one survives. REFUTED half — clusterwrite.go:1225-1229. 'It rides the forward payload so the LEADER applies the same fence the single-node writer does' is a true description of the code as written: line 1234 does populate Sid, and the leader does apply the scoped predicate. The reviewer's objection ('true only while the payload is populated') is not a claim the comment makes; it is L-F1 restated as a comment defect. A comment is not required to warn that its invariant is untested, and this one never says PlanMarkExited fails closed. CONFIRMED half — cluster_forward.go:217-218: 'the cross-session fence (proc.MarkExited) holds on the forwarded path too' names a function that is never called on the forwarded path (the dispatch arm at cluster_forward.go:726 calls proc.PlanMarkExited) and that would in fact REJECT the empty sid the surrounding sentence is about (proc.go:1
- **对修法的批评**：The cluster_forward.go half of the fix is correct and costs nothing: name proc.PlanMarkExited, and say plainly that PlanMarkExited accepts the empty value while MarkExited refuses it. The clusterwrite.go half should be rejected as written — it proposes replacing an accurate sentence with one that names a test ('<TestName> asserts the field is populated here'). Do not write that until the test exists, and if L-F1's guard lands as an AST gate the reference should name the gate file the way the other gate anchors do (`// gate-control:` / `// origin:` style), not an inline promise. A comment that names a nonexistent test is precisely the failure test/determinism's promised-guard check exists to catch (CLAUDE.md §5: 'notes 里点名的测试必须存在'), so landing that sentence before the test would turn a MINOR comment nit into a red gate.
- **建议修法**：In clusterwrite.go, replace the guarantee with the condition: 'the leader applies the same fence ONLY while this payload carries the sid — PlanMarkExited treats an absent one as an N-1 peer and renders the pre-fence predicate, so <TestName> asserts the field is populated here', pointing at the test L-F1 asks for. In cluster_forward.go, name proc.PlanMarkExited rather than proc.MarkExited, and say that PlanMarkExited is the writer that accepts the empty value while MarkExited refuses it.

## 4. 被驳回的 finding（2）

这一节要留着——它阻止下一轮把同样的东西再发现一遍。

### G5 · `internal/agent/session_generation_test.go:167` — REFUTED

- **原主张**：TestPtyIntakeIsSessionScopedNotPerRun is a source-text scan that a never-called function satisfies, and no test anywhere sends a PTY keystroke — so the L3-F2 relocation of `.in`/`.resize` has zero behavioural coverage.
- **驳回理由**：The load-bearing evidence is factually wrong. The finding states `grep -rn 'SubjPtyIn|SubjPtyResize' --include=*_test.go` finds no publisher other than test/p5 — it does: test/cli_e2e/run_modes_test.go:93 publishes `proto.SubjPtyResize(sid, res.PID)`, and TestRunResizePropagatesViaSIGWINCH (run_modes_test.go:195) is a full end-to-end assertion: it runs `sh -c 'sleep 0.4; stty size'` at 80x24, fires a 200x60 resize 200ms after attach, and requires '60 200' or '200 60' in the child's output. That child only sees the new size if the agent's `.pty.*.resize` subscription received the message and called `sess.SetSize` — and that subscription is installed by startPtyIntake itself (run.go:872-889), the same function whose call the finding proposes to de-wire. So the stated failure scenario ('nothing turns red') is false: wrapping `startPtyIntake(a, nc, fin)` in `if false` reddens TestRunResizePr

### F-T9 · `internal/broker/transfer_reconcile.go:315` — REFUTED

- **原主张**：BT-F4 changed the reaper's floor but not the gauge that reports what the reaper refuses to reap, so xferUnreapableBuckets over-counts for the first 35m08s of every broker incarnation.
- **驳回理由**：The two thresholds the finding says diverged are never applied to the same bucket, so the described over-count cannot occur. (1) xferObjectReapFloor RETURNS EARLY on the sizeAware branch: `floor := minAge; if sizeAware && floor > 0 { floor += xferCrossHomeExtraFor(size); return floor }` — the uptime term at the bottom is reached only when sizeAware is false. (2) xferBucketHasAgedObjects is called from exactly one place (transfer_reconcile.go:141), nested inside `if !b.homeOwnsXferBucket(sid)` AND `if b.selfID != "" && b.xferBucketOrphanedEverywhere(sid)` — split-home/zero-node buckets in CLUSTER mode only. The reap on that branch is `reapBucketObjects(ctx, name, b.crossHomeReapAge(), true, ...)`, i.e. sizeAware=TRUE, so BT-F4's uptime term never runs there. The home-owned reap that BT-F4 actually changed is on the other side of a `continue` and never reaches the gauge. There is therefore

## 5. 完备性 critic：没有任何 lane 看过的东西

critic 自行跑命令找「谁都没看的面」，是本轮最有价值的一个 agent——它抓到的三条里有两条是
**主进程自己引入的回退**。

### CC-1 · `internal/brokermetrics/metrics.go:214, internal/clusteroffline/restore.go:427` · BLOCKER

- **主张**：The tree under review does not compile. Two files in the §3 MINOR sweep use a package they never import: metrics.go declares `var buf bytes.Buffer` with no `bytes` import, and restore.go uses `syscall.O_NOFOLLOW`/`syscall.ELOOP` four times with no `syscall` import. Because internal/broker/broker.go imports brokermetrics and cmd/tether imports clusteroffline, the failure cascades to the two largest packages in the product.
- **失败场景**：`go build ./...` on the current working tree emits `internal/brokermetrics/metrics.go:214:11: undefined: bytes` and four `undefined: syscall` lines; building internal/broker and cmd/tether individually both FAIL. Therefore `make test`, `make lint`, `make e2e-parallel` and `make gates` — CLAUDE.md §5's 提交前硬闸, every one of which is required green before this batch can be called done — cannot have passed, and cannot pass now. Concretely: `tether` does not build, so no e2e matrix, no CLI, no broker. The mtimes show why the review missed it: the two broken files were written at 07:30, after install.sh (06:21) and proc.go (03:08), i.e. the sweep landed on top of a tree that lanes had already read. proc-exit-fence reports `go test ./internal/proc/ ./internal/broker/` BOTH ok at 348s — that run predates the sweep and is no longer reproducible. lease-farewell saw the break but misattributed it to a rogue agent's in-flight edits and filed it as 'not a finding'; it is batch content, and it is the state the batch is in.
- **建议修法**：Add `"bytes"` to internal/brokermetrics/metrics.go's import block and `"syscall"` to internal/clusteroffline/restore.go's, then re-run the full §5 gate set (`make test` + `make e2e-parallel` + `make lint`) against the final tree — not against the tree as it stood when each lane read it. Treat the whole batch's green-gate claim as unverified until that run exists.

### CC-2 · `internal/brokermetrics/metrics.go, internal/clusteroffline/{backup,offline,restore}.go, internal/clusterroster/fetch.go, internal/logrotate/logrotate.go, internal/testharness/leakgate.go` · MAJOR

- **主张**：The §3 MINOR sweep is a seventh production batch that no lane was assigned and that has no test at all — not a weak test, none. Reverting all seven behaviour changes leaves every gate green. Two of the seven are security-shaped (root-run O_NOFOLLOW on the restore stager, credential redaction in the forensic dump) and one changes the repo's own shared leak gate.
- **失败场景**：Any regression in these seven hunks ships silently, and CC-1 is the proof that this is not hypothetical: the batch shipped in a state that does not compile, and the only lane that noticed filed it as somebody else's problem. Concretely, each is unguarded on its own terms — the /metrics 500-on-panic path (a bare recover previously returned 200 with a truncated body, so Prometheus alerts evaluated against no-data and failed OPEN) can revert to fail-open with no test going red; the O_NOFOLLOW on copyFileSync, which the comment correctly calls a privilege boundary because `sudo tether cluster recovery restore` runs as root against a tether-writable data dir, can be dropped and nothing notices; redactSecretColumns can stop redacting proxy_subscribers.psk and the forensic dump silently becomes a plaintext copy of every subscriber's live proxy password again. internal/testharness/leakgate.go is the worst placed of the seven: it is the shared FD-leak gate every suite in the repo calls, and its new FDCountFailed=-2 branch — the one that turns 'the process is out of descriptors' from a silent PASS into a failure — has no test either, so the gate's own most important behaviour is now itself u
- **建议修法**：Give the sweep a lane (or fold its seven files into the existing lanes explicitly), and add at least one guard per changed behaviour, each mutation-verified per the repo's own rule that a new guard must go red against the defect it claims to catch: an httptest scrape whose snap() panics must observe 500 not 200; a copyFileSync call through a planted symlink must observe the ELOOP refusal; a dump of a proxy_subscribers row must observe the redaction marker; a FDCount()==FDCountFailed input to AssertNoFDLeak must observe t.Errorf. All five packages already have test files to host these.

### CC-3 · `internal/tunnel/tunnel.go:130 (maxRegisterHandshakes), :412-470 (acceptLoop)` · MAJOR

- **主张**：internal/tunnel/tunnel.go is the largest production hunk in the batch (267 lines) and had no owning lane — guard-vacuity mutation-tested two of its guards and comments-and-invariants read its prose, but nobody reviewed its logic. Inside it, the new maxRegisterHandshakes=256 ceiling has zero tests and converts a memory-pressure DoS into a much cheaper fleet-wide availability DoS on the one listener the deployment guide tells operators to expose to the public internet.
- **失败场景**：The ceiling is global (not per-IP), the slot is taken in acceptLoop before the goroutine starts, and because `ln` is a TLS listener the handshake happens on first Read inside handleAgent under the 5s read deadline — so a peer that completes a bare TCP connect and then sends nothing holds a slot for the full 5 seconds. An attacker holding 256 idle TCP sockets open against :7000 keeps the channel permanently saturated. Every subsequent connection is refused at `default:` with `registerHandshakeRefused` + `conn.Close()` — no DENY line, just an EOF the agent cannot distinguish from a network blip. Effect: no agent anywhere in the fleet can register a new or recovering expose; already-installed sessions survive because they are past the ceiling, so the broker looks healthy and `tether ps` looks normal while every `tether expose` and every agent reconnect silently fails. Cost to the attacker: 256 sockets. Before this change the same flood cost the broker goroutines and fds but legitimate agents still got through.
- **建议修法**：Assign tunnel.go a lane. For the ceiling specifically: add a test that opens maxRegisterHandshakes+1 connections and asserts the (N+1)th is refused — that is the guard that is missing today regardless of the design decision. Then decide the design deliberately: a per-remote-IP sub-cap so one source cannot consume the global budget, and/or a much shorter pre-line deadline than 5s (the legal REGISTER line is one write; 5s is a slow-loris budget, not a handshake budget). Both belong in the same file as the constant, whose comment already frames the listener as hostile.

### CC-4 · `internal/cluster/snapshot.go:253` · MAJOR

- **主张**：The headline half of the cluster-fsm L3-F1 fix — prefer `f.localID`, an id raft itself knows, over re-reading the identity from the very database the restore is about to overwrite — is completely unguarded. Every test that drives restoreFrom goes through the `selfID == ""` DB-fallback branch, because the test helper that builds the fsm never sets localID.
- **失败场景**：Mutating `selfID := f.localID` to `selfID := ""` — i.e. reverting to exactly the pre-fix identity source that the comment says caused split brain — leaves internal/cluster and test/cluster both green. So the split-brain regression the fix exists to prevent can come back (by a refactor dropping the field, by a fifth `&fsm{}` construction site forgetting to set it, or by a straight revert) with no test going red. The failure it hides is the one the comment describes: a joiner whose live cluster_meta was corrupted mid-restore adopts the LEADER's raft ServerID, the next InstallSnapshot re-reads and 'preserves' the wrong id so the damage is self-confirming, and the next restart brings up two servers claiming one id. cluster-fsm noticed the precondition — it wrote 'All three production sites now set localID; the helper does not' — but drew no conclusion from it, and guard-vacuity read restore_identity_test.go and cleared it, so the observation fell between the two lanes.
- **建议修法**：Give freshFSM an id (or add a freshFSMWithID variant) and add one test that discriminates the two sources: seed the joiner's live cluster_meta.self_node_id with a WRONG value (say the leader's id, simulating the interrupted-restore state the comment describes), set f.localID to the joiner's real id, run InstallSnapshot, and assert the installed DB holds f.localID and not the DB value. That test is red under the mutation above and green under the fix, which is the discrimination the current pair lacks.

### CC-5 · `scripts/install.sh:740,744,890-943,1019-1147` · MAJOR

- **主张**：The broker half of the install.sh change is structurally untestable and was not tested by anything: install_broker hardcodes /etc/tether and /etc/systemd/system with no PREFIX seam, so `go test` cannot reach its four config_dest sites; no lane ran a simcluster drill; and the deploy-release lane states outright it never executed the broker role. This is exactly the case CLAUDE.md §5 reserves the drills for — 只在改动真实部署栈（install.sh / nats.conf / systemd unit）时跑 — and install.sh changed by 148 lines including every unit-file and nats.conf write.
- **失败场景**：A defect in any of the four broker-side config_dest sites is undetectable until a real broker install, on a script that generates root-owned config from unquoted heredocs — the class that already produced defect P9, where backticks in a comment were executed by root and silently deleted two words from an installed unit file. And the mechanism has a live contradiction that only shows on a real machine: on an upgrade re-run the units are KEPT and the new content lands as *.service.new, but install_broker still prints '✔ systemd units created and ENABLED for boot (nats-server, tether-broker, caddy)' — a claim that is false for every existing machine — with report_kept_configs emitted only AFTER that success banner, where an operator who has already read the ✔ will not look. So a release that must correct a unit file reaches zero existing machines while the installer reports success. install.sh's own FORCE_CONFIG comment names this exact scenario ('a release that has to correct a unit file (the way G1 #23 shipped Restart=always) relies on the rewrite to reach existing machines') and mitigates it with the banner that the ordering defeats.
- **建议修法**：Two separable things. (a) Run the relevant simcluster drill before this ships — this is the change class the harness exists for, and `test/simcluster/README.md`'s drills 51 (full DR, real install.sh path) and 95 (broker self-heal, asserts on install.sh unit content) both already install a broker. (b) Give install_broker the same PREFIX seam install_agent has for ETC_DIR/SYSTEMD_DIR so test/p10 can drive the broker branch at all, and move report_kept_configs ABOVE the ✔ banner (or fold the kept-file list into the banner text) so the success claim and what actually happened cannot disagree.

### CC-6 · `internal/clusteroffline/backup.go:82` · MINOR

- **主张**：The new flock in OfflineBackup is taken BEFORE the live-daemon interlock it sits above, so on a cluster broker it now answers first — and with a worse message. The in-code comment asserts the opposite ordering: 'It does NOT make the backup refuse while the broker is running: it already does, two lines below.' The interlock is two lines below, but the flock is above it, and a running cluster broker holds that same lock for its entire lifetime.
- **失败场景**：An operator runs `tether cluster backup --offline` while the broker is still up — a normal mistake in a disaster-recovery moment, and the exact mistake checkNoLiveDaemon was written to catch helpfully. Instead of the interlock's guidance they now get `cluster: data-dir lock held (/var/lib/tether/tether.lock): resource temporarily unavailable`, which does not say the broker is the holder or that stopping it is the fix. Secondary: OfflineBackup now WRITES to the data dir (AcquireDataDirLock O_CREATEs tether.lock and chowns it), so a backup is no longer a read-only operation on its source — a backup pointed at a read-only mount or a snapshot copy now fails where it used to succeed. Neither behaviour is covered: reverting the whole hunk leaves internal/clusteroffline green (37s, 22 test files).
- **建议修法**：Either move the AcquireDataDirLock call below checkNoLiveDaemon so the friendlier interlock answers first (the concurrent-offline-tool race the flock closes is unaffected by the ordering, since both checks run before any read), or wrap ErrDataDirLockHeld here with the same 'if a previous broker is still running, stop it first (systemctl stop tether-broker)' text broker.go:928 already carries. Either way add one test in backup_test.go that holds the lock and asserts the message the operator sees — the hunk currently has none.

## 6. 主进程裁决与处置

> 用户裁定：**第 2 轮修完即停，取消第 3 轮**。以下是第 2 轮 91 条 finding 的最终处置。
> 每条修复都做了**变异验证**（注入该守卫声称能抓的缺陷，确认它变红）；未做变异验证的守卫不计入"已修"。

### 6.1 结果

| 处置 | 条数 |
|---|---|
| 已修 + 变异验证 | **90** |
| 经论证 REFUTED / INVALID（不修） | 2（G5、F-T9） |
| **保留为 OPEN（有明确理由与关闭路径）** | 1（F-T1，见 §6.5） |
| 待用户裁定 | **0** —— 曾有一条（`_INBOX` 的 ctl 半边），已被 §6.6 推翻并实现，N-1 例外整条撤销 |

⚠ **本节 §6.1–§6.5 记录的是第 2 轮 91 条 finding 的处置；§6.6–§6.7 是其后一次
**未经内审**的大改动**（`_INBOX` 空间重设计 + `session create` 准入），它推翻了 §6.9 的原裁决。
外审请把它当作**新增量**读，而不是第 2 轮的一部分——它没有经过 step 4 的多专家对抗审查。

四硬闸在全部修复落盘后同时全绿：`make test` / `make lint` / `make gates`
（含 simcluster hermetic 闸集）/ `make e2e-parallel`（99 单元，3m57s，ALL PASS）。
另跑 deploy-tier drill `32-install-lifecycle`（本轮改了 148 行 install.sh）：
**GREEN，54 assertions，0 gaps** —— 这一条正是 CC-5 点名要求的。

### 6.2 本轮改了产品行为的（不只是补测试）

| ID | 改了什么 |
|---|---|
| **E2** | farewell 的持有者检查在 `leaseHolder` 冷 map 时默认放行 ⇒ 每次 broker 重启/re-exec/换 leader 后都重新打开 BC-F3 的洞。改为向**活着的持有者取证**（claim-probe）：只有拿到"另一个 instance 正在应答"这种**正面反证**才拒绝，其余一切（无人订阅、超时、应答者就是发送者）仍放行 —— 只会收紧，不会破坏冷 map 必须放行的那一半。 |
| **AC-1** | 归因门 `errors.Is(poolErr, nats.ErrNoServers)` 换成 `!isAuthFailure(poolErr)`。nats.go 只在最后一次拨号是 "connection refused" 时才改写成 ErrNoServers；**普通宕机 broker 给的是 `i/o timeout`**，于是 AC-F3 的修复在它最该生效的场景里从未运行，且结果取决于拒绝方在 pool 里的位置。 |
| **AC-2** | 无条件 `dropLease` 前加**持久性阈值**（3 次连续拒绝）。整集群重启时每个 broker 都会短暂回 `Authorization Violation`，旧代码在**第一次**就降级 —— 而 dropLease 会重挂 state store 并把隧道指向 basename，在共享 home 上就是让 clone 拿到 incumbent 的写权限。不能简单按 `everAuthed` 门控：持有 lease 的 agent 必然已认证过，那样会让降级在它唯一存在的场景里失效。 |
| **AC-5** | 归因探测改用**独立的 probe option 集**：摘掉本会话的 Disconnect/Reconnect/Closed 回调、关掉重连与 RetryOnFailedConnect、缩短超时、不共享 session 的 connTracker。保留身份（不带凭据的探测证明不了凭据是否被拒）。原先探测连接带着**当前代际**的回调，代际守卫拦不住它。 |
| **H-2** | upgrade 锁改为**租约感知**：`upgradeMarkerHeldByOther` 与 acquire 预检都不再把"marker 存在"当作"有人持有"，并新增 `expire-lock-lease`（additive op），HALT 时的 orchestrator 主动交还租约、**保留 marker**。于是 HALT 消息承诺的 "fix and re-run" 与紧急回滚**立刻**可用，而 join/retire 仍被 marker 围栏挡住。⚠ 修的过程中发现 SQL **优先级 bug**：`NOT A AND B` 被解析成 `(NOT A) AND B`，会让守卫比预期更严 —— 由新测试抓到。 |
| **E3** | `handleProcEvent` 两处 audit-dedup 预读改用 `proc.GetInSession`。原来调**无 sid 作用域**的 `proc.Get`，于是 ack 码本身就是一个跨 session 的 pid 存在性 oracle，**不需要任何写**。exit 臂现已完全不可区分；started 臂的**残留**是 schema 层面的（`processes.pid` 是全局主键），已在 `proc.go` 如实写明，关闭它需要改键的迁移。 |
| **J2** | `isAgentDaemonInvocation` 只看 `args[2]`，于是 `tether agent --log-level debug doctor`（cobra 真的路由到 doctor）仍被当成守护进程并花掉升级的 boot budget。改为**按 cobra 的 stripFlags 语义**找第一个位置参数；值型 flag 表由 `TestAgentValueTakingFlagsMatchTheRealCommand` 走真实命令树对账，防止手抄表腐化。 |
| **G-5** | 重试预算 key 从 `op_id` 改成 `(op_id, step)`。批 G 把它从 1 个调用点扩到 9 个却没扩 reset 点，于是它的语义从"这一步一直失败"悄悄变成"这个 op 在任何地方失败了 5 次" —— 一次分别丢了 AddNonvoter / seed converge / lock release / AddVoter（每次重试都成功且**向前推进**）的 join，会在第 5 次被 BLOCKED。 |
| **G-6** | manual alert 的 `--label` 按 `maxAlertTextLen`(4096) 校验、却**铸造**出 dedup_key，而 ack 侧上限是 256 —— 两数之间的每个 label 都能 raise 出一条**永远 ack 不掉**的告警。改为校验**铸造出来的 key**，并让 `alert clear` 用同一个上限。 |
| **G-7** | leader 侧的 alert 存在性检查加 `CaughtUp()` 门。刚当选、正在重放长尾的 leader 会读不到已提交的告警，把合法 ack 变成**永久拒绝**；现在返回可重试的 transient。 |
| **I-F4** | 目的文件权限位的 `chmod` 从**打开时**移到**提交时**（`Fchmodat` 对着 pinned dirFD + 自己的 tmp 名）。原先整个传输期间目标目录里都躺着一个 group/world-writable 的 tmp，而 sha256 算的是**流**不是文件 —— 这个窗口里被替换的字节会以"已校验"的身份提交。 |
| **I-F5** | `.in` 回调直接读 `sess.Master` 而 `procsMu` 已释放，与 `Close()` 的 `s.Master = nil` 是真实 data race（`-race` 可复现）。新增 `pty.Session.WriteInput`，在锁内读指针、锁外写。`Hangup()` 早就为这条 race 做了 workaround 并在注释里点名 run.go —— 只是没人给 `Close()` 做。 |
| **I-F6** | `.in` / `.resize` 是**会话通配**，于是同 session 的每个 agent 都收到别的节点的原始击键流（本车队的击键包括跳板机上手打的密码）。新增**节点作用域** subject + agent ACL 钉到自己的 nid；ctl 双发，legacy 那份带 `Tether-Pty-Node` 头，新 agent 据此丢弃 —— 四种新旧组合（新ctl×新agent、新ctl×旧agent、旧ctl×新agent、跨节点）逐一有测试钉住。 |
| **K-F9** | `.new` 边车文件**从来没人清理**：`--force-config` 之后它变成 live 文件的副本（而那一轮不 keep 任何东西，报告也就不再提它），`--uninstall` 之后它是永久孤儿。两处都清理。⚠ 顺带修掉一个**我自己引入的**危险：`uninstall_broker` 里四个**硬编码绝对路径**的 `rm -rf`，在我加了 `TETHER_INSTALL_ROOT` 测试缝之后会删掉**真实**的 `/var/lib/tether`（含不可再生的 secrets）。 |
| **L-F1** | 集群路径（**车队实际跑的那条**）的 sid 围栏此前没有任何守卫：从 `ProcMarkExitedPayload` 里删掉 `Sid: sid` 就静默重开跨 session 逃逸，四闸全绿。新测试端到端跑真实 marshal → 真实 Forwarder → 真实 `SubscribeClusterApply` → 真实 leader apply。 |
| **F-T7** | boot 时的 eager finalize 仍以 `b.clusterMode` 门控，于是它存在的意义（crash-loop 快过 XferReapInterval）在 #57 刚扩展到的单 broker 上够不着。去掉门控。 |
| **CC-2** | §3 MINOR sweep 七个文件**一个测试都没有**。补上四个安全/正确性相关的：/metrics panic 必须 500（原先 fail-OPEN 返回 200，告警静默）、restore stager 的 `O_NOFOLLOW`（root 跑，双向符号链接越权）、取证 dump 的 psk 脱敏、以及**泄漏门自身**的 `FDCountFailed` 分支（"进程 fd 耗尽"从静默 PASS 变成失败 —— 一个自己没有测试的门是最坏的位置）。 |

### 6.3 空守卫族（守卫存在但抓不到它声称能抓的缺陷）

`AC-3/G4`（`attributeDialFailure` 没有任何东西断言它被接进 `connectNATS`）、`AC-4`（只测了
Disconnect 回调，而 AC-F2 讲的是 Reconnect 造成的**双重派发**）、`H-1`（chmod 0400 让**第一条**
语句就失败，于是"两条语句同一事务"对**非事务实现**同样通过 —— 改用 BEFORE DELETE trigger 让
**第二条**失败）、`J3`（探针码 `store_error` 的 class 恰好等于未分类兜底，删掉冒号切分照样绿）、
`J4`（把 broker 的 5s 预算**抄成字面量**，且不断言常量被接进 `ackAlert`）、`J7`（硬编码 5 文件、
只匹配单行 `fmt.Errorf` —— 改成整包 AST 走查）、`G2/K-F10/CC-5`（BLOCKER B4 讲的是 **broker**
角色，而四个守卫只跑 `--role agent`；加了 `TETHER_INSTALL_ROOT` 测试缝后，broker 的四个
`config_dest` 站点**逐个**变异验证过）、`G6`（ClientListen fail-closed 守卫按构造无法被任何既有
测试触达）、`G9/I-F7`（`--force` 模式保留两侧都没测；写测试时发现我自己的 setuid fixture 用了
`os.Chmod(dst, 0o4755)` —— Go 的 FileMode 不是 Unix mode word，这个测试**每次都在自我 skip**，
靠变异验证抓出来）、`G10/F-T8`（单模式合成终态既没测试、其去重论证也只是被"声称"）。

### 6.4 失实的注释与文档（改的是"说法"，不是行为）

`C7`（两条 ✔ 横幅声称写了这一轮**没有写**的文件）、`C8`/`J6`（usage.md §9.13 与
`exitcode.go` 都还写着"77=权限仅限 broker-RPC 命令"，而 `exec` 的拒绝已经走分类器）、
`C9`（architecture §E.6 仍记着"argon2 记忆硬性本身就限住吞吐"这条**已被推翻**的 v1 论证 ——
它约束的是攻击者每次猜测的成本，不是被攻击 broker 每次验证的成本）、`C10`（175 处
`// origin: prerelease audit …` 锚点里有 5 个 id **哪儿都查不到**；补了 plan §8 索引，并新增
`TestPrereleaseAuditAnchorsResolve` 机械守住）、`A-F5`（隔离单位是 **nkey** 不是机器，而 lease
特性刻意让多机共用一把 nkey）、`A-F7`（`nc.NewInbox()` 的理由注释成立，但没有任何东西会因为
改回去而变红 —— 补了行为测试 + AST 接线守卫）、`K-F12`（`SameRelease` 的极性在这个站点是**反**
的：它加宽的是**拒绝**，粘贴过来的注释把因果说反了）、`L-F3`、`F-T6`。

### 6.5 仍然 OPEN 的一条（有理由、有关闭路径）

**F-T1**：单模式的 staged-terminal 重放。改法本身可行，但会产生**重复终态**，而
`terminal_failure_outbox_test.go` 按名字拒绝它 —— "每次传输恰好一条真终态"是更强的不变量，
所以选择保留后者并把理由写进代码。它的关闭路径是明确的：把 F-T2 已经落地的**内容派生
`Nats-Msg-Id`** 接到 audit 发布路径，并把 `PubAck.Duplicate` 读作"已提交"。
（F-T2 本身已修：我原来的安全性论证——"JetStream 会折叠字节相同的 payload"——**是错的**，
JetStream 只按 `Nats-Msg-Id` 头去重；`TestSingleModeSyntheticTerminalCommitsOnceAndOnlyWhenDurable`
的变异验证实测了这一点。）

### 6.6 曾经「待用户裁定」的那一条 —— 已推翻并实现

**结论先行：`_INBOX` BLOCKER 可以在保住 N-1 的前提下彻底关闭。本 release 没有 N-1 例外。**

此前本节写的是「ctl 侧无兼容修法，已作为唯一成文例外写进 requirements §6.7」。用户要求把它研究清楚，
三个独立 agent（NATS 能力面 / 断裂面取证 / 替代设计对抗评估）从不同方向**推翻了这个结论**，并同时
指出当时的实际状态比报告所说的**更糟**。

#### 6.6.1 先说被推翻的两件事

1. **「洞已经关上了，只剩 N-1 代价」是假的。** `legacyInboxCutoff = ""` 让 `agentNeedsLegacyInbox`
   无条件返回 true，所以**每个 agent 仍然拿到裸 `Sub "_INBOX.>"`**；而拿到 agent 模板不需要是真 agent：
   匿名者三步即可——建自己的 session（`handleSessionCreate` 零准入）→ 用自己刚设的 PIN provision 一个
   agent → 拿到 agent 模板。更糟的是 `agentNeedsLegacyInbox` 在「读不到 release_version」时 **fail-open**，
   而「刚 provision、还没 register」正是攻击者的状态，所以**即使把 cutoff 设成非空这条路依然通**。
   ctl 那一半的「成文断裂」因此一分钱都没买到。
2. **「必须识别旧 ctl」是伪前提。** 它成立的条件是「legacy 授权必然是新方案授权的**超集**」，
   而那只在私有 inbox 位于 legacy 通配符**之内**时为真——那是 `permissions.go` 自己选的形状
   （原注释「THE SHAPE IS LOAD-BEARING: the prefix is a SUBTREE OF `_INBOX`」）。

#### 6.6.2 排除掉的死路（均有实证，非推理）

| 方向 | 结论 |
|---|---|
| permission **subject templates**（`{{name()}}` 等） | 只在 **operator 模式 + scoped signing key** 下展开（`auth.go:991`/`auth_callout.go:240`）。tether 是 conf 模式 ⇒ 不展开，当字面 token。且**所有**模板变量都取自签发方数据，无一来自客户端运行时。 |
| **response permissions**（`allow_responses`） | 只作用于 **publish**：记账在 `deliverMsg`、消费在 `pubAllowedFullCheck`；`canSubscribe` 完全不引用 `c.replies`。与 subscriber 侧无关。 |
| **subject mapping / transforms** | account 级、只改**入站 publish** 的 subject、不感知连接，且不改 reply 字段。 |
| **分 account** | 实测跨账户回复被改写到 `_R_.<rand>`，而 `canSubscribe` 对 `_R_.>` **没有保留检查** ⇒ 回复方账户里任何连接都能订到全部回复。account **不天然隔离回复**；且 conf 模式无法把用户放进动态创建的 account。 |
| 靠 **token 数**用窄 allow 区分 | 实测 `allow _INBOX.*.*` 会**接受**订阅 `_INBOX.*.>` 并投递 4 段消息（权限匹配把订阅串自己的 `*`/`>` 当字面 token）。**所以 deny 是必需的。** |
| CONNECT 阶段的**版本信号** | auth_callout 请求的全部字段里没有一个能可靠区分新旧 ctl；inbox 前缀在 CONNECT 里**根本不出现**（nats.go 首次 Request 时才本地生成）。这一半原结论是**对的**。 |

#### 6.6.3 定稿设计（用户裁定：设计 C）

**让两个空间不相交**，于是「声称自己是旧版」拿到的不是**更多**权限，而是**另一块**空间，
里面只有旧二进制自己放进去的流量。自报版本因此可以安全接受。

- **私有前缀改三段** `_INBOX.<a>.<b>` ⇒ 新客户端每个主题 ≥4 段（两段前缀不行：多路复用回复恰好是
  三段，与 legacy 回复同深）。
- **legacy 授权** = `allow _INBOX.*` + `_INBOX.*.*`，**`deny _INBOX.*.*.>`**。deny 在**投递路径**
  （mperms）生效，跨 route 同样成立。
- **连接标记** `auth.InboxCapableMarker` 走 `ConnectOptions.Username`；旧 broker 不读它 ⇒ **broker
  回滚方向也通**。四个混配象限全通。
- **一个 helper**（`internal/natsinbox.InboxConnectOptions`）同时给出前缀与标记，因为只设其一会
  静默失败；`test/architecture/inbox_prefix_pairing_test.go` 机械守住。
- **为什么选 C 而不是「前缀移出 `_INBOX`」**：后者 ACL 更简单，但要给 broker 的**静态 nats.conf 用户**
  加 `Pub`，而 `topology_reconcile.go:51` 在 `b.cl == nil` 直接返回 ⇒ **单机 broker 永不重渲染
  nats.conf**，install.sh 重跑又是 KEEP + 写 `.new`。那会让升级后的单机 broker 每条 ctl 命令都断。
  C 全部留在 `_INBOX.>` 之内，broker 已有的授权直接覆盖，**零 nats.conf 改动**。
- **删除**：`agentNeedsLegacyInbox` / `legacyInboxCutoff` / `node.LastReleaseVersion`（标记取代查表，
  且更强——查表在首次 join 时 fail-open，正是攻击者的状态）。
- **requirements §6.7 的 N-1 例外整条撤销。**

**变异验证（真 nats-server，`test/d3`）**：前缀退回 2 段、或拿掉 deny，
`TestLegacyGrantCannotReachAModernInbox` 立即读到 `"tunnel-token-and-every-psk"`。
**这同时实测证明了本 release 原本的 2 段前缀确实在漏。**

**残留，如实说明**：窗口内尚未升级的 ctl / agent，其回复仍在浅空间里、对任何声称旧版的连接可读。
这**是 N-1 窗口这个承诺的内涵**（不可能既继续服务一个把 inbox 放在共享空间的二进制，又让它的回复
对共享空间不可见），不是对它的违反。每个主体的暴露在**它自己升级的那一刻**结束。

#### 6.6.4 顺带堵掉的两个洞

- **broker 自己的连接没有私有 inbox**（`brokerConnectOptions` 无 `CustomInboxPrefix`）：它的请求回复
  全落在共享浅空间，而今天每个 agent 都有 `_INBOX.>` ⇒ **每个 NAT 侧 agent 都能读 broker 的全部请求回复**。
  另有两处包级 `nats.NewInbox()` 加了前缀也不跟着走，一并改为 `nc.NewInbox()`。
- **两个 ctl 模板多余的 `Pub "_INBOX.>"`**：ctl 从不向 inbox 发布（`PublishRequest` 只需要**服务
  subject** 的 Pub 权限），而未激活模板发给任何持合法 nkey 的连接 ⇒ 等于让公网可以往每条连接的回复
  空间里写。唯一在行使它的是 test/d3 里一个把它当「随便一个被允许的 subject」的正面对照。

### 6.7 `session create` 零准入（用户裁定：本轮一起做）

上面那条三步链的**根**。`handleSessionCreate` 此前没有任何准入检查，所以本审计中每一处
「activated member / provisioned agent 是可信主体」的推理，谈论的其实是整个互联网；它同时波及
`sys.events` 订阅与**无配额**的 JetStream 流创建。

- `session_creators` 表（migration 0019）+ `tether admin session-allow`（root-only admin socket）。
- **升级零运维动作**：自动纳入每个已拥有 session 的 owner 指纹。全新 broker 从空表开始。
- **准入排在 argon2 之前**：这个调用点从来没被计入进程级 argon2 预算，未准入者可按请求白烧
  broker 0.15–0.3s CPU。守卫按**时间**断言（变异实测 1.03s vs 单次 210ms），因为把拒绝挪到 hash
  之后能骗过任何结构性检查。
- **集群走 raft**（新 op `SessionCreatorSet`，已写进 §5 目录）：不只是「FSM 是唯一写者」，
  更因为一个 fp 在 A broker 准入、B broker 没有，就等于「ctl 碰巧连到哪台」决定它能不能建 session。
- **读侧不经 raft**（本地视图）：准入是策略不是凭据，慢一个 apply 可接受；读失败**拒绝**而非当成空表。

### 6.8 查证后判定**不是本轮引入**的两条（交外审知悉）

1. **`TestLeaseModelRandomSequencesHoldTheInvariants` 在并行矩阵下偶发**。该文件第 42 行自述
   「farewell（ReleasingName）路径不建模」——而本轮改的正是那条路径；它用真实时间
   （`backgroundProbeBudgetOverride` 压到 45ms）对抗 CPU 争抢。本树与干净 HEAD 各 15 轮均绿，
   重跑 `make e2e-parallel` 亦绿。属 `parallel-flake-rootcause.md` 记载的那一类，**本轮未修**。
2. **drill `60-user-journey` 的两条断言失败**（`J-G.3c-2` / `J2`）。在**同样负载**下用干净 HEAD 的
   worktree 复跑，**同样两条、逐字相同**。两条都标着「single check, no poll」，而本机常驻实验负载
   （单进程 ~230% CPU）。**本轮未修**，但它是先于本轮存在的 drill 脆弱点。

### 6.9 （历史）曾经的「需要用户裁定」原文 —— 已被 §6.6 推翻，保留以便对照

> 以下是本节被推翻前的原文。保留它是因为「当时为什么会这样判」比结论本身更值得下一个人读。

**`_INBOX` BLOCKER 的 ctl 半边（A-F1 / AC-6 / G1）无法在保住 N-1 的前提下关闭。**

- **agent 侧已有兼容修法且已实现**：broker 依 `nodes.release_version` 决定是否仍补发裸
  `_INBOX.>`（`internal/authcallout/handler.go` 的 `agentNeedsLegacyInbox`，cutoff 常量
  `legacyInboxCutoff` 当前为 `""` ⇒ 所有 agent 都算 legacy，**行为与今天逐字相同**；
  决定 cutover 版本时只改这一个常量）。
- **ctl 侧没有兼容修法**：ctl 自选 inbox 前缀，任何自报版本都可伪造，broker 铸权限时没有
  可信证据区分新旧 ctl。已直接去掉 member 模板的裸通配符，代价是**上一个 release 的 ctl
  二进制拿不到回复**（失败是响亮的：请求超时，不是静默的不安全）。
- 已按要求把这条写进 `docs/requirements.md §6.7` 作为**本次唯一的成文 N-1 例外**（A-F6）。
- **若您更希望 bump `ProtoVersion` 当纪元断裂处理**（architecture §21.4 许可），这条要推翻重做，
  且那会让整个车队必须重装而非 upgrade。请裁定。


---

## 7. 增量 2 内审（`_INBOX` 根分离 + `session create` 准入）

> §6 是**第 2 轮**的报告，本节是**增量 2** 的。两者的对象不同：§6.6/§6.7 记录的是增量 2
> 的**设计与实现**（当时未经任何内审），本节是对那份实现的**对抗性审查与裁决**。
> §6.6 里关于 `_INBOX` 的设计描述已被本节推翻——保留原文以便对照，但**不要照它实现**。

### 7.0 这轮怎么跑的，以及**没有覆盖到什么**

| 阶段 | agent 数 | 完成 |
|---|---|---|
| Recon（共享事实底图） | 5 | 5 |
| Review（16 条固定 lane） | 16 | 16 |
| Verify（每 lane 无条件 3 个镜头：refute / exploit / impact） | 48 | 48 |
| Empirical（隔离副本里真跑变异、真跑 N-1 互操作、真跑 migration） | 6 | 6 |
| Critique（覆盖面 / 诚实性 / 流程合规 / 发布风险） | 4 | **0** |
| Report（综合 + 独立审计综合） | 2 | **0** |

**最后 6 个 agent 没有跑成**：会话额度在 2h12m / 1630 万 token 处耗尽。已完成的 75 个 agent
全部把结构化结果落盘（`raw/` 75 份 JSON），一份未丢；缺的是**跨 lane 的去重、诚实性打假、
覆盖面批评，以及一份独立于主进程的综合**。resume 没能补上：本仓的 workflow 缓存 key 不是
`(prompt, opts)` 的纯内容哈希——逐字节相同的 prompt（65389 字节、sha256 相同）在两次运行里
拿到不同的 key，于是并发 fan-out 出来的 48 个 verify 会整批重跑。**所以本节的综合与裁决是
主进程做的，没有第二双眼睛复核**——这是本轮最大的方法学缺口，外审应当据此调高对 7.2/7.3
的怀疑度。

**原始材料规模**：16 条 lane 报出 114 条 finding，48 个验证者另报 80 条，6 个实证 agent 另报
37 条，合计 **231 条**（未去重）。5 个侦察 agent 另报出约 30 条与简报不符的事实。

**这轮没有覆盖到的**（如实列出，不是谦辞）：

- **跨 lane 去重与诚实性打假没有独立执行者**。231 条里明显同源的（例如 raft 能力门被 6 条
  独立报出、migration 0019 被 8 条独立报出）是主进程手工归并的。
- **deploy-tier**：`test/simcluster` 的 drill 一个都没跑。准入的**滚动升级时序**这一段只有
  hermetic 覆盖，`docs/reviews/simcluster-coverage-inventory.md` 里那一行已改判为
  `owner=drill 60-user-journey（待补臂）`——**臂还没补**。
- **真集群的 N-1 端到端**：能力门与回填的正确性由单元与行为测试钉住，但没有起过一个
  真的混版 raft 集群跑一次 `session-allow`。
- **可观测性 / metrics / 审计事件**面：准入的授予与撤销**不产生任何审计事件**（L8-F7），
  本轮未修，理由见 7.4。

### 7.1 结论

设计层面出了一条 BLOCKER 级错误，而且它**不是实现 bug，是前提错误**：`_INBOX` 隔离押在一条
`deny` 上，而那条 deny 在现网 pin 的服务端上**从未被装载过**。另外两条 BLOCKER 分别是「公钥即
凭据」（既有缺陷，但增量 2 的两条新保证完全建立在它之上）和「破坏性操作门 fail-open」。

| | CONFIRMED | REFUTED / 已被覆盖 | 合计 |
|---|---|---|---|
| blocker | 3 | 0 | 3 |
| major | 14 | — | 14 |
| minor / nit | 21 | — | 21 |
| 归并掉的同源条目 | — | ~190 | — |

「归并」不等于「驳回」：231 条原始条目里绝大多数是同一缺陷被多条 lane 独立报出，归并后的
38 条已全部处置。**没有一条被判为「不修」**，除 7.4 明确记录的 3 条。

### 7.2 BLOCKER

#### B1 — `_INBOX` 隔离在现网 pin 的 nats-server 上完全不生效

**报出**：`ops-upgrade/L16-F1`（单条，实证 agent 用两台真服务端复现）。
**主进程复核**：独立复现，并扩到四个版本。

增量 2 的隔离靠 `deny _INBOX.*.*.>` 兜住兼容授权。nats-server 只在**订阅串与某条 deny 子句
存在特定关系时**才为该连接装载消息级 deny 过滤器，而这个判据在上游变过：

```
<= v2.12.x   server/client.go   subjectIsSubsetMatch(denyClause, subscription)
>= v2.14.0   server/client.go   SubjectsCollide(denyClause, subscription)
```

`subjectIsSubsetMatch("_INBOX.*.*.>", "_INBOX.<字面量>.>")` 为 **false**（第 2 段 deny 侧是 `*`、
订阅侧是字面量），于是过滤器**从未装载**，该字面量之下的每一条四段私有回复照常投递。

主进程用本仓自己的 allow/deny 列表、每个版本一台真服务端实测：

```
2.10.22  sub _INBOX.aaaaaaaa.>  ** DELIVERED **  data="tunnel-token-and-every-psk"
2.11.0   sub _INBOX.aaaaaaaa.>  ** DELIVERED **
2.11.9   sub _INBOX.aaaaaaaa.>  ** DELIVERED **
2.12.0   sub _INBOX.aaaaaaaa.>  ** DELIVERED **
2.14.0   sub _INBOX.aaaaaaaa.>  not-delivered (deny held)
```

`scripts/install.sh` / `Makefile` / `broker-ops.md` / simcluster 镜像当时全部 pin **v2.10.22**；
`go.mod` 里那台 **v2.14.0** 只喂 `make test` 的嵌入式服务端。**所以每一条 hermetic 断言量的都是
一台没人跑的二进制。** 这个坑本仓已经踩过一次并记在 `docs/reviews/INDEX.md`（2026-08-06
smalldisk：「先把 go.mod 的 nats-server v2.14.0（嵌入式测试 server）当成生产的 v2.10.22」）。

**并且在 `_INBOX.` 之内、服务端 < 2.14 时这个性质做不到**，不是没写好：旧客户端的请求多路
复用器必须要三段通配授权（`_INBOX.<nuid>.*`），而任何三段通配授权都同时准入
`_INBOX.<字面量>.>`；服务端在那个位置分不出 `*` 与 `>`。没有任何 deny 列表能成为
`_INBOX.<任意字面量>.>` 的子集。

**修法（用户裁定：A + B + 回退，全做）**

1. 私有 inbox 移到**自己的顶层根** `auth.InboxRoot = "_TINBOX"`，前缀 `_TINBOX.<a>.<b>`。
   两个空间在**第一段**就不相交，与服务端版本无关。`LegacyInboxAllow` 退回朴素的
   `_INBOX.>`，deny 整条删除。
2. 响应者两个根都要 `Pub`（agent 模板、`PermissionsForBroker`，且**不得**按自身 vintage 条件化
   ——请求方的 vintage 与响应方无关）。
3. `{旧 broker × 新客户端}` 这一格：旧 broker 只铸 `Sub _INBOX.>`，新客户端订 `_TINBOX.…` 会被
   **拒绝**。`internal/natsinbox.Connect` 连上后**自探一次**自己的 inbox，被拒就退回浅前缀重连。
   探测是**有序的、不是计时的**：服务端按顺序处理 SUB 与 Flush 的 PING，nats.go 的 readLoop
   在 `processPermissionsViolation` 里**同步**设置 `LastError()`，所以 Flush 返回时拒绝已可观测。
   代价是每条连接一个 PING/PONG 往返。
4. 服务端 pin 三处对齐 **v2.14.5**（v2.14.6 会把 go directive 抬到 1.26，而 CLAUDE.md 锁 1.25），
   `install.sh` 由「已存在即跳过」改为**按版本比对后 rename 替换并要求重启**，
   新增 `test/architecture/nats_server_pin_test.go` 钉住「测的服务端 == 发的服务端」。

**验证**：新设计在 2.10.22 / 2.12.0 / 2.14.0 三台真服务端上，legacy 授权穷举 10 种逃逸形状
（`_TINBOX.>` / `_TINBOX.<victim>.>` / `>` / `*.>` / 字面主题 …）**全部在 SUBSCRIBE 期被拒**
（响亮的 `-ERR`，不是投递侧过滤），正控制通过。四个变异（前缀放回 `_INBOX` / legacy 同时拿到
两个根 / helper 只设前缀 / helper 只设标记）全部变红。

#### B2 — auth_callout 从不验证 CONNECT 的 nonce 签名：公钥即凭据

**报出**：`admission-enforcement/L9-F1` 与 `red-team/REFUTE-F1` 独立报出，两者都端到端复现。

`internal/authcallout` 只检查 `ConnectOptions.Nkey` 是不是语法合法的 user 公钥，**全仓零处**
校验 `SignedNonce`。而 nats-server 在 conf 模式下**不替 callout 验它**：`server/auth.go` 发现该
nkey 不在静态 `s.nkeys` 里就直接 `return false`，跳过 Verify 分支，随后把 Nkey / SignedNonce /
Nonce 原样转给 callout。本包自己的 package doc 三个 release 以来第一句写的正是相反的话
（「NATS server has already verified the client's nkey signature」）。

**后果不是抽象的**：`clientNkey` 是每个权限模板的 actor——它锚定 `ctrl.by.<actor>` 的发布授权、
派生 `auth.InboxPrefixFor` 的私有子树，而准入表键的就是从该 subject 解出的 actor 的指纹。
公钥不是秘密：`sessions.go` 把 owner 的完整公钥作为 `actor` 字段 pubSysEvent 到
`tether.v2.sys.events`，而任一 session 成员都被授权订阅它。所以「知道某人的公钥」就等于
「能读他的私有 inbox（含 agent register reply 里的 tunnel token 与全部 PSK）」加上
「能以他的身份通过准入建 session」——增量 2 新装的准入门被完整绕过。

**修法**：`verifyClientNkey` 在 `clientNkey` 语法校验之后、任何角色分支之前验签；nonce 或签名
为空一律 deny（**不能**降级成空模板：nats-server 里空 `jwt.Permissions` 等于 `c.perms=nil`，
即完全不受限，比现状更糟）。解码顺序与服务端一致（RawURL 优先、Std 回退）。

**N-1 无影响**：四个生产拨号点本来就签。代价是 `internal/authcallout` 与 `test/d3` / `test/d4`
里手工构造 claims 的单测需要补签名——这些测试此前构造的是**任何真实客户端都不会产生的形状**。
助手改成从注册表取私钥真签，取不到就**响亮失败**而不是静默变成未签名请求。

**验证**：新增 `TestAConnectionCannotPresentSomebodyElsesUserNkey`（受害者公钥 + 攻击者签名
必须被拒；无签名必须被拒；正控制：本人签名必须通过）。拿掉验签调用 → 冒名连接被放行、守卫变红。

#### B3 — 破坏性操作门 fail-open：「问不出来」被读成「集群健康」

**报出**：`pairing-sweep/IMPACT-F1`。

`probeClusterHealth` 把**订阅被拒 / 发布被拒 / 超时 / N=1 无应答**统统折叠成空切片，而
`proto.EvalDestructiveGate(nil)` 是**不拦**。于是一条 inbox 被拒的 ctl 在「什么都没问出来」
的情况下继续执行 `session rm` / `cluster retire`——安全门在它本该防住的那种配置错误上失效。
一次更早的外审（H3/H-4）看见了这个折叠，但只在**一个**调用点用「数应答者」绕过去；本条是
那个绕法一直在替代的根修。

**修法**：`probeClusterHealth` 返回 `([]ClusterHealthResp, error)`，`(nil,nil)` = 没人应答、
`(nil,err)` = 问不出来。11 个调用点按 fail-open 风险分流：`gateDestructive`（拒绝并给出
77/69 两种退出类）、`canaryCommandVerCheck`（HALT）、`upgradeLockHeld`（报「持有」而不是
「已清」）、`countVoters`（返回 error）、`probeLocks`（0 应答者）；其余显示/轮询类走
新的 `probeClusterHealthAdvisory`，那条 helper 的 doc 写明「每个调用方在空结果上必须**做得更少**」。

**这里犯过一次错，写下来**：第一版修法只在 `SubscribeSync` 返回 error 时报错——而权限违例是
**异步**到达的，`SubscribeSync` 返回 nil。新写的守卫第一次就抓到了这个（gate 依然放行）。
最终用与 `natsinbox.Connect` 相同的有序 Flush + `LastError()` 判定。

### 7.3 MAJOR

| # | 问题 | 修法 |
|---|---|---|
| M1 | 新 raft op `OpSessionCreatorSet` **没有混版能力门**；旧 voter 对未知 op **不是 fail-stop 而是静默 poison-skip**，而第 2 层文档写的是 fail-stop | 复用 v0.4.2 phase-fluidity 的机制并**提取为共享的** `checkVotersSupportOps`；新增 `HasSessionCreatorOps` + `ClusterHealthResp.SessionCreatorOps`（additive/omitempty，零值=不支持=拒绝）；`setSessionCreator` 与启动回填都过门。文档改正。**6 条 lane 独立报出** |
| M2 | migration 0019 的回填是**各节点本地从另一张复制表派生**：滚动升级必然分歧且永不收敛；**且每次重跑都复活被 `--remove` 撤销的指纹**（撤销刻意不删 session）；`CURRENT_TIMESTAMP` 逐副本发散 | migration 只建**空表**；回填改为 broker 启动时的**一次性、经 raft** 的 `seedSessionCreators`，marker 与数据行在**同一条 raft entry** 里；单机走同一事务。**8 条 lane 独立报出** |
| M3 | adminsock 的 `SessionCreatorWrite` seam **fail-open**：集群模式下 seam 未接线就落到直写路径 | 补 `ClusterMode` fail-closed 分支（与 `OpEvict` 同形、同理由） |
| M4 | 集群模式 `--remove` 无条件报 "removed"，即使指纹从来不在表里 | 复制型 DELETE **不能**报告删没删；改为如实说明并让 CLI **以限定语打头**而不是「removed X + 括号里的反话」 |
| M5 | `session-allow` 接受任何字符串当指纹并报 admitted | `auth.ValidFingerprint`；坏格式是 **bad request**。真实触发路径是从 `admin sessions` 的**截断 OWNER 列**粘贴 |
| M6 | 全新部署被卡住：准入步骤在 `usage.md` / `broker-ops.md` / `install.sh` 里**一个字都没有**，而唯一指引点名了**不存在**的 `tether whoami` | 新增 `tether whoami`（离线，打印指纹+公钥）；`usage.md` §5 与 `broker-ops.md` §2.3/§5.20 各补一节 |
| M7 | `not_allowed` 落在未分类的 **exit 70**，而 `usage.md` §9.13 让自动化把 70 当**退避重试**——这是永久拒绝 | `SessionCreateResp` 加 `Code`（additive/omitempty，旧 broker 由 ctl 按前缀回退）；`not_allowed` → **77 EX_NOPERM** |
| M8 | 配对闸门只守「谁能设前缀」，**不守 helper 自己是不是两半都设**；扫描对别名导入/点导入/结构体字面量/字段直写全盲；报错串指向**不存在**的 `auth.InboxConnectOptions` | 三件事全守；扫描按选择器名匹配；marker 读方进**死条目即红**的账本 |
| M9 | 包级 `nats.NewInbox()` 的守卫只盯 5 个站点中的 1 个，且长在 `exit_class_family_test.go`（与内容无关的文件） | 提升为仓库级架构闸门；删掉错位的那个。broker 侧那两个站点是**静默**失败模式（它仍持 `_INBOX.>`，用错 helper 照样跑），正是原守卫够不到的那两个 |
| M10 | `PlanSetCreator` 绕开 `cluster.LitTime` 烤 RFC3339Nano：同一列两种文本编码，破坏 §13.2/DIFF-1 等价，`ORDER BY added_at` 比较两套字母表 | 改用 `LitTime`。**守卫比的是 `quote(added_at)` 的原始存储文本**——驱动读出时会归一化，比读回值等于什么都没比（第一版守卫就是这样，变异不红才发现） |
| M11 | `internal/session/creators.go` **零测试文件**；复制路径、回填、三条写在注释里的行为承诺全部无覆盖 | 新增 `creators_test.go`：准入语义、幂等且如实报告、**撤销不删 session**、两条写路径产出同一行、回填只跑一次且不复活撤销、复制型回填 marker 同 entry。四个变异全红 |
| M12 | `test/d3` 的「包级 inbox 被拒 / 连接自身 inbox 可用」两条断言**都是反的**，靠异步 error 记录器分不清哪次违例而保持绿灯 | 连接改走 `natsinbox.InboxConnectOptions`（它此前正是新闸门禁止的半接线形态），两条断言随之成真 |
| M13 | 兼容授权无到期机制/计数器/日志字段，运维无从判断何时可退役 | 未实现计数器（见 7.4），但 requirements §6.7 的残留段落改写为如实说明：窃听者不需要任何凭据，且浅空间里流过的是**长寿凭据**，升级后依然有效、全仓无一处轮换 |
| M14 | 拒绝路径每次写一条不受限速的 `Warn`，攻击者可任选 nkey，构成匿名日志放大器 | 降为 `Debug`。拒绝本身是控制，日志行不是 |

### 7.4 明确**不修**的（3 条，各有理由）

1. **准入的授予/撤销不产生审计事件**（L8-F7）。`admin session-allow` 走 root-only 0600 socket，
   本轮已把它接进 raft，所以**变更本身在 raft 日志里**。再加一条 `sys.events` 需要决定它对
   哪些主体可见（该 subject 是全局的，且 B2 刚证明「session 成员」不是可信集合）——那是一个
   独立的设计问题，不该塞进本轮。**记为后续项。**
2. **兼容授权的使用计数器**（L1-F5 的一半）。要正确实现必须区分「真旧客户端」与「声称旧版的
   攻击者」，而设计的前提恰恰是**不需要**区分。一个数不清语义的计数器会被读成安全信号。
3. **窗口期泄漏凭据的轮换**（requirements §6.7 新写的残留段落点名了它）。tunnel token 与
   subscriber PSK 是长寿凭据，升级不会作废它们。轮换是一条**独立的、跨 agent 的**变更，
   本轮不做，但已在 requirements 里写成一条尚未实现的后续项而不是留白。

### 7.5 已被复核推翻或降级的

- **侦察阶段报「URL 带 userinfo 会静默吞掉 marker、降级成 legacy」**——三条独立复核推翻：
  nats.go 会把 nkey 与签名一起丢掉，结果是连接期**响亮的** Authorization Violation，不是静默降级。
- **「半接线 ⇒ 每个请求静默超时、线上和日志里都没有任何线索」**——四处文档（含 CLAUDE.md 闸门表、
  `natsinbox` 自身的注释）如此写，**实测是响亮的**：SUB 期 `-ERR`、服务端日志、nats.go 异步错误。
  这条假前提正是「用源码闸门而不用运行时自检」的理由，四处全部改正，且 `natsinbox.Connect`
  现在两者都做。
- **`test/d3` 受 `d3_integration` tag 门控**（两份侦察产物这么记）——该 tag 从未存在。
- **`TestLeaseModelRandomSequencesHoldTheInvariants` 与 drill `60-user-journey` 的两条断言**——
  §6.8 已查证不是本轮引入，本轮复核维持该判定。

### 7.6 实证收据

| 实证 | 做了什么 | 结果 |
|---|---|---|
| ACL 判定台 | 独立于本仓测试自建最小程序，三台真服务端 × 10 种逃逸形状 × 正控制 | 见 7.2 B1 |
| inbox ACL 变异 | 8 种注入 | 主进程复跑 4 种关键变异，全红 |
| 准入/闸门变异 | 9 种注入 + `taskset` 限 2 核复核时间断言 | 时间断言在 2 核下仍有裕度 |
| N-1 互操作 | v0.5.1 二进制 × 新 broker | 报出 6 条，其中 exit 70 与 `whoami` 两条已修 |
| migration 0019 | 7 种 DB 起始态 | 报出「回填零测试」「滚动升级分歧」等，已由 M2 修 |
| 按包 `-race` | 15 个包 | 报出 `js_placement_gate_test.go` 自我 skip（非本轮，未修） |

|---|---|---|
| `test/architecture/testdata/structural_budget_golden.txt` | `pkg-code-lines cmd/tether` 10000→12000；`pkg-files cmd/tether` 56→57 | +1 文件是 `whoami.go`（帮助文本早就让运维跑它、而它不存在）；跨过 2000 量化边界的是它加上本轮在 `alert_gate.go` / `session.go` / `admin.go` / `error_hints.go` 补的注释。**债务上限**已连同下一步偿还路径写在 golden 里 |
| `internal/natsconf/testdata/*.conf`（4 份） | broker 静态用户 Pub/Sub 各 +`"_TINBOX.>"` | clustered broker 的 nats.conf **就是**那份权限列表；reconciler 下一次 tick 会换 conf 并 SIGHUP，这是预期且必要的 |
